local function computeLadderVelocity(p136, p137, p138, p139) -- Line: 497
    -- upvalues: getViewBasis (copy)
    local LadderSpeed = p139.LadderSpeed;

    if p136.Duck or p136.Walk then
        LadderSpeed = LadderSpeed * p139.LadderClimbModifier;
    end;

    local v140 = math.clamp(-p136.MoveVector.Y, -1, 1);
    local v141 = math.clamp(p136.MoveVector.X, -1, 1);
    local v142 = v140 * LadderSpeed;
    local v143 = v141 * LadderSpeed;

    if math.abs(v142) <= 1e-6 and math.abs(v143) <= 1e-6 then
        return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0);
    end;

    local v144, v145 = getViewBasis(p136.LookYaw, p136.VerticalLook);
    local v146 = v144 * v142 + v145 * v143;
    local v147 = (Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)):Cross(p137);
    local v148 = v147.Magnitude <= 1e-6 and Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) or v147.Unit;
    local v149 = p137:Cross(v148);
    local v150 = v146:Dot(p137);
    local v151 = p137 * v150;
    local v152 = v146 - v151;
    local v153 = v149:Dot(v152);
    local v154 = v148:Dot(v152);
    local v155 = v148 * v154 + v151;

    if v155.Magnitude > 1e-6 and v155.Unit:Dot(p137) < p139.LadderDampenAngle then
        v152 = v149 * v153 + v148 * (p139.LadderDampen * v154);
    end;

    local v156 = (v152 - v149 * v150) * p139.LadderLateralScale;

    if p138 and v150 > 0 then
        v156 = v156 + p137 * p139.LadderSpeed;
    end;

    return v156;
end;

local function resolveGroundDriveDirection(p299, p300, p301) -- Line: 1101

local function walkMove(p514, p515, p516, p517, p518, p519, p520, p521, p522, p523, p524, p525, p526, p527) -- Line: 2096

    local v529 = X * X + Z * Z;

local function resolveCorrectedPositionAgainstLocalWorld(p221, p222, p223, p224, p225, p226) -- Line: 933
    -- upvalues: u6 (copy), Diagnostics (copy)
    local MovementCollisionWorld = p221.MovementCollisionWorld;

    if MovementCollisionWorld == nil then
        return "missingCollisionWorld", nil, nil;
    end;

    if typeof(p226) == "number" and MovementCollisionWorld.SetSimulationTime ~= nil then
        MovementCollisionWorld:SetSimulationTime(p226);
    end;

    local v227 = p224.DuckAmount >= 0.999 and "crouching" or "standing";

    if MovementCollisionWorld.SetSimulationPlayerPosition ~= nil then
        MovementCollisionWorld:SetSimulationPlayerPosition(p224.Position);
    end;

    local v228 = MovementCollisionWorld:PointTest(p224.Position, v227, u6);
    local v229 = {
        stance = v227,
        serverPositionPointTest = Diagnostics.SerializeCorrectionTrace(v228)
    };

    if p224.WallNormal.Magnitude > 1e-6 then
        v229.serverWallProbe = Diagnostics.SerializeCorrectionTrace(MovementCollisionWorld:Sweep(p224.Position, p224.Position - p224.WallNormal.Unit * 0.25, v227, u6));
    end;

    if p223.Magnitude > 1e-6 then
        v229.clientWallProbe = Diagnostics.SerializeCorrectionTrace(MovementCollisionWorld:Sweep(p222, p222 - p223.Unit * 0.25, v227, u6));
    end;

    v229.serverGroundProbe = Diagnostics.SerializeCorrectionTrace(MovementCollisionWorld:SupportSweep(p224.Position, p224.Position - Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), v227, p225, nil, u6));
    v229.clientGroundProbe = Diagnostics.SerializeCorrectionTrace(MovementCollisionWorld:SupportSweep(p222, p222 - Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), v227, p225, nil, u6));

    if not (v228.startSolid or v228.allSolid) then
        return "serverPositionClear", nil, v229;
    end;

    local v230 = MovementCollisionWorld:Sweep(p222, p224.Position, v227, u6);
    v229.approachTrace = Diagnostics.SerializeCorrectionTrace(v230);

    if v230.startSolid or v230.allSolid then
        return "approachStartedSolid", nil, v229;
    end;

    local Position = p224.Position;
    p224.Position = v230.endPos;

    return "adjustedToLocalBoundary", p224.Position - Position, v229;
end;

local function buildPlanesForPoints(p107, p108) -- Line: 319
    -- upvalues: QuickHull (copy), addPlane (copy)
    assert(#p107 >= 4, "A convex movement hull requires at least four points");
    local v109 = QuickHull.GenerateHull(p107);
    assert(#v109 > 0, "QuickHull returned no triangles");
    local v110 = {};

    for _, v in ipairs(v109) do
        local v111 = (v[1] - v[2]):Cross(v[1] - v[3]);

        if v111.Magnitude > 0.001 then
            local Unit = v111.Unit;

            if addPlane(v110, Unit, v[1]:Dot(Unit), p108 + 1) then
                p108 = p108 + 1;
                v110[#v110].planeNum = p108;
            end;
        end;
    end;

    assert(#v110 > 0, "QuickHull returned no usable planes");

    return v110, p108;
end;

local function buildLocalHullMinkowskiPlanes(p172, p173, u174, u175, u176) -- Line: 527
    -- upvalues: QuickHull (copy), addPlane (copy), u158 (copy)
    local v177 = QuickHull.GenerateHull(p173);
    local v178 = {};

    for _, v in ipairs(v177) do
        local v179 = (v[1] - v[2]):Cross(v[1] - v[3]);

        if v179.Magnitude > 0.001 then
            local Unit = v179.Unit;
            local v180 = true;

            for _, v2 in ipairs(v178) do
                if (v2 - Unit).Magnitude <= 0.001 then
                    v180 = false;
                    break;
                end;
            end;

            if v180 then
                table.insert(v178, Unit);
            end;
        end;
    end;

    local u181 = {};

    local function v186(p182, p183, p184) -- Line: 554
        -- upvalues: u174 (copy), u175 (copy), addPlane (ref), u181 (copy), u176 (ref)
        if p182.Magnitude <= p183 then
            return;
        end;

        local Unit = p182.Unit;
        local v185 = (-1 / 0);

        for _, v in ipairs(u174) do
            v185 = math.max(v185, v:Dot(Unit));
        end;

        if addPlane(u181, Unit, v185 + (math.abs(Unit.X) * u175.X + math.abs(Unit.Y) * u175.Y + math.abs(Unit.Z) * u175.Z), u176 + 1, p184) then
            u176 = u176 + 1;
        end;
    end;

        v186(p172:VectorToWorldSpace(v), 0.001, "sourceFace");
    end;

    for _, v in ipairs(u158) do
        v186(v, 0.001, "playerAxis");
        v186(-v, 0.001, "playerAxis");
    end;

    for i = 1, #v178 do
        for i2 = i + 1, #v178 do
            local v187 = v178[i]:Cross(v178[i2]);

            if v187.Magnitude > 0.02 then

                    v186(v189, 0.02, "edgeBevel");

                end;

        end;
    end;

    return u181, u176;
end;

        v136 = getAuthoritativeEquippedName();

    end;

        v138 = v137.WalkSpeed;
    else
        v138 = nil;

        weaponName = v136,
